<div class="field__item"> <section aria-label="" class="union-difference">
<div class="field field--name-field-body-section field--type-entity-reference-revisions field--label-hidden field__item"> <div class="container">
<div class="l-66-33--1">
<h1></h1>
<div class="field field--name-field-body field--type-text-long field--label-hidden field__item"><h1>UNION ALUMNI MAKING A DIFFERENCE</h1>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="container">
<a class="union-difference-grid__link l-3up" href="/after-union/alumni-profiles/law-career-service">
<div class="union-difference-grid__block">
<div class="union-difference-grid__desc">
<h2 class="union-difference-grid__title">
<div class="field field--name-field-title field--type-string field--label-hidden field__item">A law career of service</div>
</h2>
<div class="field field--name-field-body field--type-text-long field--label-hidden field__item"><p>"Pro bono work is very important to me because it allows me to assist underserved individuals, organizations and communities that would not otherwise have access to legal representation."</p>
</div>
<span class="union-difference-grid__cta">Carla Reeves '08</span>
</div>
<div class="union-difference-grid__img" style="background-image: url('/sites/default/files/union-marketing-layer/201908/carla-reeves.jpg')"></div>
</div>
</a>
<a class="union-difference-grid__link l-3up" href="/after-union/alumni-profiles/theater-therapy">
<div class="union-difference-grid__block">
<div class="union-difference-grid__desc">
<h2 class="union-difference-grid__title">
<div class="field field--name-field-title field--type-string field--label-hidden field__item">Theater as therapy </div>
</h2>
<div class="field field--name-field-body field--type-text-long field--label-hidden field__item"><p>"Children with autism have difficulty reading nonverbal cues, communicating, and expressing emotions. Improv requires them to do all of these things. In addition, it is always safe."</p>
</div>
<span class="union-difference-grid__cta">Rachel Magin '14</span>
</div>
<div class="union-difference-grid__img" style="background-image: url('/sites/default/files/union-marketing-layer/201908/r-magin-two_0.jpg')"></div>
</div>
</a>
<a class="union-difference-grid__link l-3up" href="/after-union/alumni-profiles/helping-refugees-balancing-lifes-scales">
<div class="union-difference-grid__block">
<div class="union-difference-grid__desc">
<h2 class="union-difference-grid__title">
<div class="field field--name-field-title field--type-string field--label-hidden field__item">Helping refugees</div>
</h2>
<div class="field field--name-field-body field--type-text-long field--label-hidden field__item"><p>“We are just scratching the surface of what’s possible in terms of how technology can be leveraged to help the most vulnerable live a safer, healthier, and more empowering life.”</p></div>
<span class="union-difference-grid__cta">Mike Clarke ’11</span>
</div>
<div class="union-difference-grid__img" style="background-image: url('/sites/default/files/union-marketing-layer/201908/mark-clarke.jpg')"></div>
</div>
</a>
